{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD2qVxgdMgRvjhDq2qZW","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now the TipJar contract:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":506,"cache_read_input_tokens":23906,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":506},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b64972d3-9143-428e-99ba-009c8fd3c974","uuid":"06872cce-faa9-439b-9f19-49de7a2e9ffe","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:12:11.602Z","request_id":"req_011CeD2qVA4oiVFA3ywwFDby"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD2qVxgdMgRvjhDq2qZW","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0159kwTLfKaKWu3jRwgm3eaf","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T000854Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/contracts/src/TipJar.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.24;\n\nimport {IERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport {SafeERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\n\n/// @title TipJar\n/// @notice A tip jar that accepts USDC tips. Each tip carries an optional\n///         short message and is recorded on-chain so a public \"tip feed\" can\n///         be rendered by any client. The jar owner can withdraw the balance.\n/// @dev The USDC token address is supplied at construction time so the same\n///      contract works against real USDC on Base\n///      (0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913) or a mock token locally.\ncontract TipJar {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    /// @notice Longest message (in bytes) a tip may carry.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES = 280;\n\n    /// @notice The ERC-20 token accepted as tips (USDC, 6 decimals).\n    IERC20 public immutable token;\n\n    /// @notice The address allowed to withdraw collected tips.\n    address public owner;\n\n    struct Tip {\n        address from; // tipper\n        uint128 amount; // token amount, in the token's smallest unit (USDC: 6 decimals)\n        uint64 timestamp; // block time of the tip\n        string message; // optional public message\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Every tip ever sent, in chronological order.\n    Tip[] private _tips;\n\n    /// @notice Running total of all tips received, in the token's smallest unit.\n    uint256 public totalTips;\n\n    /// @notice Total tipped per address, in the token's smallest unit.\n    mapping(address => uint256) public tippedBy;\n\n    event TipReceived(\n        uint256 indexed index, address indexed from, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp\n    );\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event OwnerChanged(address indexed previousOwner, address indexed newOwner);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error MessageTooLong();\n    error NotOwner();\n    error ZeroAddress();\n\n    modifier onlyOwner() {\n        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();\n        _;\n    }\n\n    constructor(IERC20 _token, address _owner) {\n        if (address(_token) == address(0) || _owner == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        token = _token;\n        owner = _owner;\n        emit OwnerChanged(address(0), _owner);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Send a tip. The caller must have approved this contract to spend\n    ///         `amount` of the token first.\n    /// @param amount  Amount of token (USDC smallest unit, 6 decimals) to tip.\n    /// @param message Optional public message (<= MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES bytes).\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (bytes(message).length > MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES) revert MessageTooLong();\n\n        // Pull the tokens in. SafeERC20 reverts if the transfer fails or the\n        // token returns false. We record the amount actually received so the\n        // feed stays correct even for fee-on-transfer tokens.\n        uint256 before = token.balanceOf(address(this));\n        token.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n        uint256 received = token.balanceOf(address(this)) - before;\n        if (received == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        uint256 index = _tips.length;\n        _tips.push(\n            Tip({\n                from: msg.sender,\n                amount: uint128(received),\n                timestamp: uint64(block.timestamp),\n                message: message\n            })\n        );\n        totalTips += received;\n        tippedBy[msg.sender] += received;\n\n        emit TipReceived(index, msg.sender, received, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips recorded.\n    function tipsCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read a single tip by index.\n    function getTip(uint256 index) external view returns (Tip memory) {\n        return _tips[index];\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read the most recent tips, newest first.\n    /// @param limit Maximum number of tips to return.\n    function getRecentTips(uint256 limit) external view returns (Tip[] memory page) {\n        uint256 n = _tips.length;\n        uint256 count = limit < n ? limit : n;\n        page = new Tip[](count);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n            page[i] = _tips[n - 1 - i];\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Current token balance held by the jar.\n    function jarBalance() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return token.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw collected tips to the owner.\n    /// @param amount Amount to withdraw (smallest unit). Pass 0 to withdraw all.\n    function withdraw(uint256 amount) external onlyOwner {\n        uint256 bal = token.balanceOf(address(this));\n        uint256 toSend = amount == 0 ? bal : amount;\n        token.safeTransfer(owner, toSend);\n        emit Withdrawn(owner, toSend);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Transfer ownership of the jar (withdrawal rights).\n    function transferOwnership(address newOwner) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newOwner == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        emit OwnerChanged(owner, newOwner);\n        owner = newOwner;\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":506,"cache_read_input_tokens":23906,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":506},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b64972d3-9143-428e-99ba-009c8fd3c974","uuid":"197f1dcc-0806-450b-a402-51753d06200e","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:12:33.668Z","request_id":"req_011CeD2qVA4oiVFA3ywwFDby"}

Each tip carries an optional\n///         short message and is recorded on-chain so a public \"tip feed\" can\n///         be rendered by any client. The jar owner can withdraw the balance.\n/// @dev The USDC token address is supplied at construction time so the same\n///      contract works against real USDC on Base\n///      (0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913) or a mock token locally.\ncontract TipJar {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    /// @notice Longest message (in bytes) a tip may carry.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES = 280;\n\n    /// @notice The ERC-20 token accepted as tips (USDC, 6 decimals).\n    IERC20 public immutable token;\n\n    /// @notice The address allowed to withdraw collected tips.\n    address public owner;\n\n    struct Tip {\n        address from; // tipper\n        uint128 amount; // token amount, in the token's smallest unit (USDC: 6 decimals)\n        uint64 timestamp; // block time of the tip\n        string message; // optional public message\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Every tip ever sent, in chronological order.\n    Tip[] private _tips;\n\n    /// @notice Running total of all tips received, in the token's smallest unit.\n    uint256 public totalTips;\n\n    /// @notice Total tipped per address, in the token's smallest unit.\n    mapping(address => uint256) public tippedBy;\n\n    event TipReceived(\n        uint256 indexed index, address indexed from, uint256 amount, string message, uint256 timestamp\n    );\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event OwnerChanged(address indexed previousOwner, address indexed newOwner);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error MessageTooLong();\n    error NotOwner();\n    error ZeroAddress();\n\n    modifier onlyOwner() {\n        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();\n        _;\n    }\n\n    constructor(IERC20 _token, address _owner) {\n        if (address(_token) == address(0) || _owner == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        token = _token;\n        owner = _owner;\n        emit OwnerChanged(address(0), _owner);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Send a tip. The caller must have approved this contract to spend\n    ///         `amount` of the token first.\n    /// @param amount  Amount of token (USDC smallest unit, 6 decimals) to tip.\n    /// @param message Optional public message (<= MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES bytes).\n    function tip(uint256 amount, string calldata message) external {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (bytes(message).length > MAX_MESSAGE_BYTES) revert MessageTooLong();\n\n        // Pull the tokens in. SafeERC20 reverts if the transfer fails or the\n        // token returns false. We record the amount actually received so the\n        // feed stays correct even for fee-on-transfer tokens.\n        uint256 before = token.balanceOf(address(this));\n        token.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n        uint256 received = token.balanceOf(address(this)) - before;\n        if (received == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        uint256 index = _tips.length;\n        _tips.push(\n            Tip({\n                from: msg.sender,\n                amount: uint128(received),\n                timestamp: uint64(block.timestamp),\n                message: message\n            })\n        );\n        totalTips += received;\n        tippedBy[msg.sender] += received;\n\n        emit TipReceived(index, msg.sender, received, message, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Number of tips recorded.\n    function tipsCount() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return _tips.length;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read a single tip by index.\n    function getTip(uint256 index) external view returns (Tip memory) {\n        return _tips[index];\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Read the most recent tips, newest first.\n    /// @param limit Maximum number of tips to return.\n    function getRecentTips(uint256 limit) external view returns (Tip[] memory page) {\n        uint256 n = _tips.length;\n        uint256 count = limit < n ? limit : n;\n        page = new Tip[](count);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n            page[i] = _tips[n - 1 - i];\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Current token balance held by the jar.\n    function jarBalance() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return token.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw collected tips to the owner.\n    /// @param amount Amount to withdraw (smallest unit). Pass 0 to withdraw all.\n    function withdraw(uint256 amount) external onlyOwner {\n        uint256 bal = token.balanceOf(address(this));\n        uint256 toSend = amount == 0 ? bal : amount;\n        token.safeTransfer(owner, toSend);\n        emit Withdrawn(owner, toSend);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Transfer ownership of the jar (withdrawal rights).\n    function transferOwnership(address newOwner) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newOwner == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        emit OwnerChanged(owner, newOwner);\n        owner = newOwner;\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD2sFmDAqB6Y2rcV4aRa","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01EsnN29nBcszoMnGgiqBLYt","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T000854Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/contracts/src/MockUSDC.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.24;\n\nimport {ERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\n\n/// @title MockUSDC\n/// @notice A 6-decimal ERC-20 that stands in for USDC on a local chain, where\n///         the real Base USDC contract does not exist. It exposes an open\n///         `mint` faucet so any local wallet can grab test funds. This is a\n///         development aid only and must never be deployed to a real network.\ncontract MockUSDC is ERC20 {\n    constructor() ERC20(\"Mock USD Coin\", \"USDC\") {}\n\n    function decimals() public pure override returns (uint8) {\n        return 6;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Faucet: mint test USDC to any address.\n    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {\n        _mint(to, amount);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2280,"cache_read_input_tokens":24412,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2280},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b64972d3-9143-428e-99ba-009c8fd3c974","uuid":"184d9d47-957f-4dbb-97e3-422717b7bcc5","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:12:40.781Z","request_id":"req_011CeD2sEusHyRLti26eHwEq"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD2snJVzKzryVNZ83KXh","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01GYsZm3JbkzS9uyYzDMVXBT","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T000854Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/contracts/script/Deploy.s.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.24;\n\nimport {Script, console2} from \"forge-std/Script.sol\";\nimport {TipJar} from \"../src/TipJar.sol\";\nimport {MockUSDC} from \"../src/MockUSDC.sol\";\nimport {IERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\n\n/// @notice Deploys the TipJar (and, for local chains, a mock USDC + a faucet\n///         top-up for the deployer), then writes the resulting addresses to\n///         `frontend/src/deployments.json` so the web app can pick them up.\n///\n/// Environment variables:\n///   USDC_ADDRESS  (optional) existing USDC/ERC-20 to accept. If unset or zero,\n///                 a MockUSDC is deployed and 1,000,000 test USDC is minted to\n///                 the deployer.\ncontract Deploy is Script {\n    function run() external {\n        uint256 pk = vm.envOr(\"PRIVATE_KEY\", uint256(0));\n        address deployer = pk == 0 ? msg.sender : vm.addr(pk);\n\n        address configured = vm.envOr(\"USDC_ADDRESS\", address(0));\n\n        if (pk == 0) {\n            vm.startBroadcast();\n        } else {\n            vm.startBroadcast(pk);\n        }\n\n        IERC20 usdc;\n        if (configured == address(0)) {\n            MockUSDC mock = new MockUSDC();\n            mock.mint(deployer, 1_000_000 * 1e6); // 1,000,000 test USDC\n            usdc = IERC20(address(mock));\n            console2.log(\"MockUSDC deployed:\", address(mock));\n        } else {\n            usdc = IERC20(configured);\n            console2.log(\"Using existing USDC:\", configured);\n        }\n\n        TipJar jar = new TipJar(usdc, deployer);\n        console2.log(\"TipJar deployed:  \", address(jar));\n        console2.log(\"Owner:            \", deployer);\n\n        vm.stopBroadcast();\n\n        _writeDeployments(address(jar), address(usdc), configured == address(0));\n    }\n\n    function _writeDeployments(address tipJar, address usdc, bool isMock) internal {\n        string memory json = string.concat(\n            \"{\\n\",\n            '  \"chainId\": ',\n            vm.toString(block.chainid),\n            \",\\n\",\n            '  \"tipJar\": \"',\n            vm.toString(tipJar),\n            '\",\\n',\n            '  \"usdc\": \"',\n            vm.toString(usdc),\n            '\",\\n',\n            '  \"usdcDecimals\": 6,\\n',\n            '  \"usdcIsMock\": ',\n            isMock ? \"true\" : \"false\",\n            \"\\n}\\n\"\n        );\n\n        string memory path = \"../frontend/src/deployments.json\";\n        vm.writeFile(path, json);\n        console2.log(\"Wrote deployments to frontend/src/deployments.json\");\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":626,"cache_read_input_tokens":26692,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":626},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b64972d3-9143-428e-99ba-009c8fd3c974","uuid":"2470e126-6d0a-42fd-94a2-a6d1db54e93d","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:12:55.615Z","request_id":"req_011CeD2sm8oGvVjxQ69KvB9X"}

If unset or zero,\n///                 a MockUSDC is deployed and 1,000,000 test USDC is minted to\n///                 the deployer.\ncontract Deploy is Script {\n    function run() external {\n        uint256 pk = vm.envOr(\"PRIVATE_KEY\", uint256(0));\n        address deployer = pk == 0 ? msg.sender : vm.addr(pk);\n\n        address configured = vm.envOr(\"USDC_ADDRESS\", address(0));\n\n        if (pk == 0) {\n            vm.startBroadcast();\n        } else {\n            vm.startBroadcast(pk);\n        }\n\n        IERC20 usdc;\n        if (configured == address(0)) {\n            MockUSDC mock = new MockUSDC();\n            mock.mint(deployer, 1_000_000 * 1e6); // 1,000,000 test USDC\n            usdc = IERC20(address(mock));\n            console2.log(\"MockUSDC deployed:\", address(mock));\n        } else {\n            usdc = IERC20(configured);\n            console2.log(\"Using existing USDC:\", configured);\n        }\n\n        TipJar jar = new TipJar(usdc, deployer);\n        console2.log(\"TipJar deployed:  \", address(jar));\n        console2.log(\"Owner:            \", deployer);\n\n        vm.stopBroadcast();\n\n        _writeDeployments(address(jar), address(usdc), configured == address(0));\n    }\n\n    function _writeDeployments(address tipJar, address usdc, bool isMock) internal {\n        string memory json = string.concat(\n            \"{\\n\",\n            '  \"chainId\": ',\n            vm.toString(block.chainid),\n            \",\\n\",\n            '  \"tipJar\": \"',\n            vm.toString(tipJar),\n            '\",\\n',\n            '  \"usdc\": \"',\n            vm.toString(usdc),\n            '\",\\n',\n            '  \"usdcDecimals\": 6,\\n',\n            '  \"usdcIsMock\": ',\n            isMock ? \"true\" : \"false\",\n            \"\\n}\\n\"\n        );\n\n        string memory path = \"../frontend/src/deployments.json\";\n        vm.writeFile(path, json);\n        console2.log(\"Wrote deployments to frontend/src/deployments.json\");\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD2tsT67DxSW3ZAEQJ5T","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now the tests:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1443,"cache_read_input_tokens":27318,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1443},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b64972d3-9143-428e-99ba-009c8fd3c974","uuid":"8369b145-70c1-40d3-9bc8-acd7f1497028","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:12:57.129Z","request_id":"req_011CeD2trdUbz2YEnfbduRQM"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD2tsT67DxSW3ZAEQJ5T","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01E2mw9BMHXCmz1wifyhrqUm","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T000854Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/contracts/test/TipJar.t.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.24;\n\nimport {Test} from \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport {TipJar} from \"../src/TipJar.sol\";\nimport {MockUSDC} from \"../src/MockUSDC.sol\";\n\ncontract TipJarTest is Test {\n    MockUSDC usdc;\n    TipJar jar;\n\n    address owner = makeAddr(\"owner\");\n    address alice = makeAddr(\"alice\");\n    address bob = makeAddr(\"bob\");\n\n    uint256 constant ONE = 1e6; // 1 USDC (6 decimals)\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        usdc = new MockUSDC();\n        jar = new TipJar(usdc, owner);\n        usdc.mint(alice, 100 * ONE);\n        usdc.mint(bob, 100 * ONE);\n    }\n\n    function _tip(address who, uint256 amount, string memory msg_) internal {\n        vm.startPrank(who);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), amount);\n        jar.tip(amount, msg_);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_TipRecordsFeedAndBalances() public {\n        _tip(alice, 5 * ONE, \"great work!\");\n\n        assertEq(jar.tipsCount(), 1);\n        assertEq(jar.jarBalance(), 5 * ONE);\n        assertEq(jar.totalTips(), 5 * ONE);\n        assertEq(jar.tippedBy(alice), 5 * ONE);\n\n        TipJar.Tip memory t = jar.getTip(0);\n        assertEq(t.from, alice);\n        assertEq(t.amount, 5 * ONE);\n        assertEq(t.message, \"great work!\");\n    }\n\n    function test_RecentTipsAreNewestFirst() public {\n        _tip(alice, 1 * ONE, \"first\");\n        _tip(bob, 2 * ONE, \"second\");\n        _tip(alice, 3 * ONE, \"third\");\n\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory page = jar.getRecentTips(2);\n        assertEq(page.length, 2);\n        assertEq(page[0].message, \"third\");\n        assertEq(page[1].message, \"second\");\n\n        // Asking for more than exist just returns everything.\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory all = jar.getRecentTips(50);\n        assertEq(all.length, 3);\n    }\n\n    function test_TipEmitsEvent() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), 7 * ONE);\n        vm.expectEmit(true, true, false, true, address(jar));\n        emit TipJar.TipReceived(0, alice, 7 * ONE, \"hi\", block.timestamp);\n        jar.tip(7 * ONE, \"hi\");\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertOnZeroAmount() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAmount.selector);\n        jar.tip(0, \"\");\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertOnLongMessage() public {\n        string memory long = new string(281);\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), ONE);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.MessageTooLong.selector);\n        jar.tip(ONE, long);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertWithoutApproval() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(); // ERC20InsufficientAllowance\n        jar.tip(ONE, \"no approve\");\n    }\n\n    function test_OwnerWithdrawAll() public {\n        _tip(alice, 10 * ONE, \"\");\n        _tip(bob, 4 * ONE, \"\");\n\n        uint256 before = usdc.balanceOf(owner);\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar.withdraw(0); // 0 == withdraw all\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), before + 14 * ONE);\n        assertEq(jar.jarBalance(), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_OwnerPartialWithdraw() public {\n        _tip(alice, 10 * ONE, \"\");\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar.withdraw(3 * ONE);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(owner), 3 * ONE);\n        assertEq(jar.jarBalance(), 7 * ONE);\n    }\n\n    function test_NonOwnerCannotWithdraw() public {\n        _tip(alice, 10 * ONE, \"\");\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.NotOwner.selector);\n        jar.withdraw(0);\n    }\n\n    function test_TransferOwnership() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        jar.transferOwnership(bob);\n        assertEq(jar.owner(), bob);\n\n        _tip(alice, ONE, \"\");\n        vm.prank(bob);\n        jar.withdraw(0);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(bob), 100 * ONE + ONE);\n    }\n\n    function test_ConstructorRejectsZeroAddresses() public {\n        vm.expectRevert(TipJar.ZeroAddress.selector);\n        new TipJar(usdc, address(0));\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1443,"cache_read_input_tokens":27318,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1443},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b64972d3-9143-428e-99ba-009c8fd3c974","uuid":"423112e2-8a2a-420e-9049-2de7cb622bef","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:13:14.949Z","request_id":"req_011CeD2trdUbz2YEnfbduRQM"}

just returns everything.\n        TipJar.Tip[] memory all = jar.getRecentTips(50);\n        assertEq(all.length, 3);\n    }\n\n    function test_TipEmitsEvent() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        usdc.approve(address(jar), 7 * ONE);\n        vm.expectEmit(true, true, false, true, address(jar));\n        emit TipJar.TipReceived(0, alice, 7 * ONE, \"hi\", test_RevertWithoutApproval() public {\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(); // ERC20InsufficientAllowance\n        jar.tip(ONE, \"no approve\");\n    }\n\n    function html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n  <head>\n    <meta charset=\"UTF-8\" />\n    <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0\" />\n    <title>USDC Tip Jar · Base</title>\n  </head>\n  <body>\n    <div id=\"app\">\n      <header class=\"topbar\">\n        <div class=\"brand\">\n          <span class=\"logo\">🫙</span>\n          <div>\n            <h1>USDC Tip Jar</h1>\n            <p class=\"subtitle\">Send a USDC tip on Base</p>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <div class=\"wallet\">\n          <span id=\"network-pill\" class=\"pill hidden\"></span>\n          <button id=\"connect-btn\" class=\"btn btn-primary\">Connect Wallet</button>\n        </div>\n      </header>\n\n      <main>\n        <section class=\"stats\">\n          <div class=\"stat\">\n            <span class=\"stat-label\">Total tipped</span>\n            <span id=\"total-tips\" class=\"stat-value\">—</span>\n          </div>\n          <div class=\"stat\">\n            <span class=\"stat-label\">In the jar</span>\n            <span id=\"jar-balance\" class=\"stat-value\">—</span>\n          </div>\n          <div class=\"stat\">\n            <span class=\"stat-label\">Tips</span>\n            <span id=\"tips-count\" class=\"stat-value\">—</span>\n          </div>\n        </section>\n\n        <div class=\"grid\">\n          <section class=\"card\">\n            <h2>Send a tip</h2>\n            <form id=\"tip-form\">\n              <label for=\"amount\">Amount (USDC)</label>\n              <input id=\"amount\" name=\"amount\" type=\"number\" min=\"0\" step=\"0.01\" placeholder=\"5.00\" required />\n\n              <label for=\"message\">Message <span class=\"muted\">(optional, ≤ 280 chars)</span></label>\n              <textarea id=\"message\" name=\"message\" maxlength=\"280\" rows=\"3\" placeholder=\"Thanks for the great work!\"></textarea>\n\n              <button id=\"tip-btn\" class=\"btn btn-primary\" type=\"submit\" disabled>Connect wallet to tip</button>\n              <button id=\"faucet-btn\" class=\"btn btn-ghost hidden\" type=\"button\">Get 1,000 test USDC</button>\n              <p id=\"wallet-balance\" class=\"muted\"></p>\n            </form>\n            <div id=\"status\" class=\"status hidden\"></div>\n          </section>\n\n          <section class=\"card\">\n            <div class=\"feed-head\">\n              <h2>Tip feed</h2>\n              <button id=\"refresh-btn\" class=\"btn btn-ghost\" type=\"button\">Refresh</button>\n            </div>\n            <ul {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD2zaJNuaoecGv3P3i8E","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now the main application logic:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":505,"cache_read_input_tokens":36344,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":505},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b64972d3-9143-428e-99ba-009c8fd3c974","uuid":"157a04b2-4b77-4163-a0c4-b30408bc62d8","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:14:26.478Z","request_id":"req_011CeD2zZN5DD1ncpVKKjXxJ"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CeD2zaJNuaoecGv3P3i8E","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_016p2kcBy4XPZiPQfA45B4Mr","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/ssd/workspace/buidlguidl/ethskills-evals-test/artifacts/frontend-playbook-goal-002/2026-08-20T000854Z-claude-no-skill-1/workspace/frontend/src/main.ts","content":"import {\n  createPublicClient,\n  createWalletClient,\n  custom,\n  http,\n  defineChain,\n  formatUnits,\n  parseUnits,\n  getAddress,\n  type Address,\n  type PublicClient,\n  type WalletClient,\n  type EIP1193Provider,\n} from \"viem\";\nimport { base } from \"viem/chains\";\nimport { tipJarAbi, erc20Abi } from \"./abi\";\nimport deployments from \"./deployments.json\";\n\nimport \"./style.css\";\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Config\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst CHAIN_ID = deployments.chainId;\nconst TIP_JAR = deployments.tipJar as Address;\nconst USDC = deployments.usdc as Address;\nconst USDC_DECIMALS = deployments.usdcDecimals;\nconst IS_MOCK = deployments.usdcIsMock;\n\n// Default local RPC. Override with ?rpc=... if you run anvil elsewhere.\nconst LOCAL_RPC =\n  new URLSearchParams(location.search).get(\"rpc\") ?? \"http://127.0.0.1:8545\";\n\n/** The chain we expect the wallet to be on, derived from the deployment. */\nconst activeChain =\n  CHAIN_ID === base.id\n    ? base\n    : defineChain({\n        id: CHAIN_ID,\n        name: CHAIN_ID === 31337 ? \"Anvil (local)\" : `Chain ${CHAIN_ID}`,\n        nativeCurrency: { name: \"Ether\", symbol: \"ETH\", decimals: 18 },\n        rpcUrls: { default: { http: [LOCAL_RPC] } },\n      });\n\nconst NOT_DEPLOYED =\n  TIP_JAR === \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\";\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Clients & state\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst publicClient: PublicClient = createPublicClient({\n  chain: activeChain,\n  transport: http(activeChain.rpcUrls.default.http[0]),\n});\n\nlet walletClient: WalletClient | null = null;\nlet account: Address | null = null;\n\nconst provider = (window as unknown as { ethereum?: EIP1193Provider }).ethereum;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// DOM helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst $ = <T extends HTMLElement>(id: string): T =>\n  document.getElementById(id) as T;\n\nconst els = {\n  connect: $(\"connect-btn\") as HTMLButtonElement,\n  networkPill: $(\"network-pill\"),\n  totalTips: $(\"total-tips\"),\n  jarBalance: $(\"jar-balance\"),\n  tipsCount: $(\"tips-count\"),\n  form: $(\"tip-form\") as HTMLFormElement,\n  amount: $(\"amount\") as HTMLInputElement,\n  message: $(\"message\") as HTMLTextAreaElement,\n  tipBtn: $(\"tip-btn\") as HTMLButtonElement,\n  faucetBtn: $(\"faucet-btn\") as HTMLButtonElement,\n  walletBalance: $(\"wallet-balance\"),\n  status: $(\"status\"),\n  feed: $(\"feed\"),\n  refresh: $(\"refresh-btn\") as HTMLButtonElement,\n  contractAddr: $(\"contract-addr\"),\n  tokenAddr: $(\"token-addr\"),\n};\n\nfunction short(addr: string): string {\n  return `${addr.slice(0, 6)}…${addr.slice(-4)}`;\n}\n\nfunction fmtUsdc(v: bigint): string {\n  const n = Number(formatUnits(v, USDC_DECIMALS));\n  return `${n.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 2 })} USDC`;\n}\n\nfunction timeAgo(ts: number): string {\n  const s = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) - ts);\n  if (s < 60) return `${s}s ago`;\n  if (s < 3600) return `${Math.floor(s / 60)}m ago`;\n  if (s < 86400) return `${Math.floor(s / 3600)}h ago`;\n  return `${Math.floor(s / 86400)}d ago`;\n}\n\ntype StatusKind = \"info\" | \"success\" | \"error\" | \"pending\";\nfunction setStatus(kind: StatusKind, msg: string): void {\n  els.status.className = `status status-${kind}`;\n  els.status.textContent = msg;\n  els.status.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n}\nfunction clearStatus(): void {\n  els.status.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Reads (feed + stats) — work without a connected wallet\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface TipItem {\n  from: Address;\n  amount: bigint;\n  timestamp: bigint;\n  message: string;\n}\n\nasync function refreshStats(): Promise<void> {\n  if (NOT_DEPLOYED) return;\n  try {\n    const [total, balance, count] = await Promise.all([\n      publicClient.readContract({ address: TIP_JAR, abi: tipJarAbi, functionName: \"totalTips\" }),\n      publicClient.readContract({ address: TIP_JAR, abi: tipJarAbi, functionName: \"jarBalance\" }),\n      publicClient.readContract({ address: TIP_JAR, abi: tipJarAbi, functionName: \"tipsCount\" }),\n    ]);\n    els.totalTips.textContent = fmtUsdc(total);\n    els.jarBalance.textContent = fmtUsdc(balance);\n    els.tipsCount.textContent = count.toString();\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error(\"stats read failed\", e);\n  }\n}\n\nasync function refreshFeed(): Promise<void> {\n  if (NOT_DEPLOYED) return;\n  try {\n    const tips = (await publicClient.readContract({\n      address: TIP_JAR,\n      abi: tipJarAbi,\n      functionName: \"getRecentTips\",\n      args: [25n],\n    })) as readonly TipItem[];\n\n    if (tips.length === 0) {\n      els.feed.innerHTML = `<li class=\"empty\">No tips yet — be the first!</li>`;\n      return;\n    }\n\n    els.feed.innerHTML = tips\n      .map((t) => {\n        const msg = t.message\n          ? escapeHtml(t.message)\n          : `<span class=\"muted\">(no message)</span>`;\n        return `\n          <li class=\"tip-item\">\n            <div class=\"tip-top\">\n              <span class=\"tip-amount\">${fmtUsdc(t.amount)}</span>\n              <span class=\"tip-time\">${timeAgo(Number(t.timestamp))}</span>\n            </div>\n            <div class=\"tip-msg\">${msg}</div>\n            <div class=\"tip-from\">from ${short(getAddress(t.from))}</div>\n          </li>`;\n      })\n      .join(\"\");\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error(\"feed read failed\", e);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction escapeHtml(s: string): string {\n  return s.replace(/[&<>\"']/g, (c) =>\n    ({ \"&\": \"&amp;\", \"<\": \"&lt;\", \">\": \"&gt;\", '\"': \"&quot;\", \"'\": \"&#39;\" })[c]!,\n  );\n}\n\nasync function refreshWalletBalance(): Promise<void> {\n  if (!account || NOT_DEPLOYED) {\n    els.walletBalance.textContent = \"\";\n    return;\n  }\n  const bal = (await publicClient.readContract({\n    address: USDC,\n    abi: erc20Abi,\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [account],\n  })) as bigint;\n  els.walletBalance.textContent = `Your balance: ${fmtUsdc(bal)}`;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Wallet connection\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync function ensureChain(): Promise<void> {\n  if (!provider) return;\n  const current = await provider.request({ method: \"eth_chainId\" });\n  if (parseInt(current as string, 16) === CHAIN_ID) return;\n\n  const hexId = `0x${CHAIN_ID.toString(16)}`;\n  try {\n    await provider.request({\n      method: \"wallet_switchEthereumChain\",\n      params: [{ chainId: hexId }],\n    });\n  } catch (err) {\n    // 4902 = chain not added to the wallet yet.\n    if ((err as { code?: number }).code === 4902) {\n      await provider.request({\n        method: \"wallet_addEthereumChain\",\n        params: [\n          {\n            chainId: hexId,\n            chainName: activeChain.name,\n            nativeCurrency: activeChain.nativeCurrency,\n            rpcUrls: activeChain.rpcUrls.default.http,\n          },\n        ],\n      });\n    } else {\n      throw err;\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nasync function connect(): Promise<void> {\n  if (!provider) {\n    setStatus(\"error\", \"No injected wallet found. Install MetaMask (or similar) to connect.\");\n    return;\n  }\n  try {\n    setStatus(\"pending\", \"Connecting wallet…\");\n    walletClient = createWalletClient({ chain: activeChain, transport: custom(provider) });\n    const [addr] = await walletClient.requestAddresses();\n    account = addr;\n    await ensureChain();\n    clearStatus();\n    onAccountReady();\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error(e);\n    setStatus(\"error\", (e as Error).message ?? \"Failed to connect wallet.\");\n  }\n}\n\nfunction onAccountReady(): void {\n  els.connect.textContent = account ? short(account) : \"Connect Wallet\";\n  els.networkPill.textContent = activeChain.name;\n  els.networkPill.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n  els.tipBtn.disabled = NOT_DEPLOYED;\n  els.tipBtn.textContent = NOT_DEPLOYED ? \"Contract not deployed\" : \"Send tip\";\n  els.faucetBtn.classList.toggle(\"hidden\", !IS_MOCK || NOT_DEPLOYED);\n  refreshWalletBalance();\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Writes (tip + faucet)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync function sendTip(evt: SubmitEvent): Promise<void> {\n  evt.preventDefault();\n  if (!walletClient || !account) {\n    setStatus(\"error\", \"Connect your wallet first.\");\n    return;\n  }\n\n  let amount: bigint;\n  try {\n    amount = parseUnits(els.amount.value.trim(), USDC_DECIMALS);\n  } catch {\n    setStatus(\"error\", \"Enter a valid amount.\");\n    return;\n  }\n  if (amount <= 0n) {\n    setStatus(\"error\", \"Amount must be greater than zero.\");\n    return;\n  }\n  const message = els.message.value.trim();\n\n  els.tipBtn.disabled = true;\n  try {\n    await ensureChain();\n\n    // 1) Approve the jar to pull USDC, if the current allowance is too low.\n    const allowance = (await publicClient.readContract({\n      address: USDC,\n      abi: erc20Abi,\n      functionName: \"allowance\",\n      args: [account, TIP_JAR],\n    })) as bigint;\n\n    if (allowance < amount) {\n      setStatus(\"pending\", \"Approve USDC spending in your wallet…\");\n      const approveHash = await walletClient.writeContract({\n        address: USDC,\n        abi: erc20Abi,\n        functionName: \"approve\",\n        args: [TIP_JAR, amount],\n        account,\n        chain: activeChain,\n      });\n      setStatus(\"pending\", \"Waiting for approval to confirm…\");\n      await publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: approveHash });\n    }\n\n    // 2) Send the tip.\n    setStatus(\"pending\", \"Confirm the tip in your wallet…\");\n    const tipHash = await walletClient.writeContract({\n      address: TIP_JAR,\n      abi: tipJarAbi,\n      functionName: \"tip\",\n      args: [amount, message],\n      account,\n      chain: activeChain,\n    });\n    setStatus(\"pending\", \"Sending tip…\");\n    await publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: tipHash });\n\n    setStatus(\"success\", `Tipped ${fmtUsdc(amount)}. Thank you! 🎉`);\n    els.form.reset();\n    await Promise.all([refreshStats(), refreshFeed(), refreshWalletBalance()]);\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error(e);\n    setStatus(\"error\", friendlyError(e));\n  } finally {\n    els.tipBtn.disabled = NOT_DEPLOYED;\n  }\n}\n\nasync function useFaucet(): Promise<void> {\n  if (!walletClient || !account) return;\n  els.faucetBtn.disabled = true;\n  try {\n    await ensureChain();\n    setStatus(\"pending\", \"Minting test USDC…\");\n    const hash = await walletClient.writeContract({\n      address: USDC,\n      abi: erc20Abi,\n      functionName: \"mint\",\n      args: [account, parseUnits(\"1000\", USDC_DECIMALS)],\n      account,\n      chain: activeChain,\n    });\n    await publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash });\n    setStatus(\"success\", \"Minted 1,000 test USDC.\");\n    await refreshWalletBalance();\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error(e);\n    setStatus(\"error\", friendlyError(e));\n  } finally {\n    els.faucetBtn.disabled = false;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction friendlyError(e: unknown): string {\n  const msg = (e as { shortMessage?: string; message?: string }).shortMessage\n    ?? (e as Error).message\n    ?? \"Something went wrong.\";\n  if (/user rejected|denied/i.test(msg)) return \"Transaction rejected.\";\n  return msg;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Wire up + boot\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction init(): void {\n  els.contractAddr.textContent = NOT_DEPLOYED ? \"not deployed\" : TIP_JAR;\n  els.tokenAddr.textContent = NOT_DEPLOYED ? \"not deployed\" : USDC;\n\n  if (NOT_DEPLOYED) {\n    setStatus(\"info\", \"No deployment found. Run the deploy script, then reload.\");\n  }\n\n  els.connect.addEventListener(\"click\", connect);\n  els.form.addEventListener(\"submit\", sendTip);\n  els.faucetBtn.addEventListener(\"click\", useFaucet);\n  els.refresh.addEventListener(\"click\", () => {\n    refreshStats();\n    refreshFeed();\n  });\n\n  if (provider) {\n    provider.on?.(\"accountsChanged\", (accs: unknown) => {\n      const list = accs as string[];\n      account = list.length ? getAddress(list[0]) : null;\n      if (!account) {\n        walletClient = null;\n        els.connect.textContent = \"Connect Wallet\";\n        els.tipBtn.disabled = true;\n        els.faucetBtn.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n      } else {\n        onAccountReady();\n      }\n    });\n    provider.on?.(\"chainChanged\", () => window.location.reload());\n  }\n\n  refreshStats();\n  refreshFeed();\n  setInterval(() => {\n    refreshStats();\n    refreshFeed();\n  }, 10_000);\n}\n\ninit();\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":505,"cache_read_input_tokens":36344,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":505},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b64972d3-9143-428e-99ba-009c8fd3c974","uuid":"fd7d8fca-82bb-4d57-831c-ba6082860126","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:15:14.168Z","request_id":"req_011CeD2zZN5DD1ncpVKKjXxJ"}

Override with ?rpc=... if you run anvil elsewhere.\nconst LOCAL_RPC =\n  new URLSearchParams(location.search).get(\"rpc\") ?? \"http://127.0.0.1:8545\";\n\n/** The chain we expect the wallet to be on, derived from the deployment. */\nconst activeChain =\n  CHAIN_ID === base.id\n    ? base\n    : defineChain({\n        id: CHAIN_ID,\n        name: CHAIN_ID === 31337 ? \"Anvil (local)\" : `Chain ${CHAIN_ID}`,\n        nativeCurrency: { name: \"Ether\", symbol: \"ETH\", decimals: 18 },\n        rpcUrls: { default: { http: [LOCAL_RPC] } },\n      });\n\nconst NOT_DEPLOYED =\n  TIP_JAR === \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\";\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Clients & state\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst publicClient: PublicClient = createPublicClient({\n  chain: activeChain,\n  transport: http(activeChain.rpcUrls.default.http[0]),\n});\n\nlet walletClient: WalletClient | null = null;\nlet account: Address | null = null;\n\nconst provider = (window as unknown as { ethereum?: EIP1193Provider }).ethereum;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// DOM helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst $ = <T extends HTMLElement>(id: string): T =>\n  document.getElementById(id) as T;\n\nconst els = {\n  connect: $(\"connect-btn\") as HTMLButtonElement,\n  networkPill: $(\"network-pill\"),\n  totalTips: $(\"total-tips\"),\n  jarBalance: $(\"jar-balance\"),\n  tipsCount: $(\"tips-count\"),\n  form: $(\"tip-form\") as HTMLFormElement,\n  amount: $(\"amount\") as HTMLInputElement,\n  message: $(\"message\") as HTMLTextAreaElement,\n  tipBtn: $(\"tip-btn\") as HTMLButtonElement,\n  faucetBtn: $(\"faucet-btn\") as HTMLButtonElement,\n  walletBalance: $(\"wallet-balance\"),\n  status: $(\"status\"),\n  feed: $(\"feed\"),\n  refresh: $(\"refresh-btn\") as HTMLButtonElement,\n  contractAddr: $(\"contract-addr\"),\n  tokenAddr: $(\"token-addr\"),\n};\n\nfunction short(addr: string): string {\n  return `${addr.slice(0, 6)}…${addr.slice(-4)}`;\n}\n\nfunction fmtUsdc(v: bigint): string {\n  const n = Number(formatUnits(v, USDC_DECIMALS));\n  return `${n.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 2 })} USDC`;\n}\n\nfunction timeAgo(ts: number): string {\n  const s = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) - ts);\n  if (s < 60) return `${s}s ago`;\n  if (s < 3600) return `${Math.floor(s / 60)}m ago`;\n  if (s < 86400) return `${Math.floor(s / 3600)}h ago`;\n  return `${Math.floor(s / 86400)}d ago`;\n}\n\ntype StatusKind = \"info\" | \"success\" | \"error\" | \"pending\";\nfunction setStatus(kind: StatusKind, msg: string): void {\n  els.status.className = `status status-${kind}`;\n  els.status.textContent = msg;\n  els.status.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n}\nfunction clearStatus(): void {\n  els.status.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Reads (feed + stats) — work without a connected wallet\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface TipItem {\n  from: Address;\n  amount: bigint;\n  timestamp: bigint;\n  message: string;\n}\n\nasync function refreshStats(): Promise<void> {\n  if (NOT_DEPLOYED) return;\n  try {\n    const [total, balance, count] = await Promise.all([\n      publicClient.readContract({ address: TIP_JAR, abi: tipJarAbi, functionName: \"totalTips\" }),\n      publicClient.readContract({ address: TIP_JAR, abi: tipJarAbi, functionName: \"jarBalance\" }),\n      publicClient.readContract({ address: TIP_JAR, abi: tipJarAbi, functionName: \"tipsCount\" }),\n    ]);\n    els.totalTips.textContent = fmtUsdc(total);\n    els.jarBalance.textContent = fmtUsdc(balance);\n    els.tipsCount.textContent = count.toString();\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error(\"stats read failed\", e);\n  }\n}\n\nasync function refreshFeed(): Promise<void> {\n  if (NOT_DEPLOYED) return;\n  try {\n    const tips = (await publicClient.readContract({\n      address: TIP_JAR,\n      abi: tipJarAbi,\n      functionName: \"getRecentTips\",\n      args: [25n],\n    })) as readonly TipItem[];\n\n    if (tips.length === 0) {\n      els.feed.innerHTML = `<li class=\"empty\">No tips yet — be the first!</li>`;\n      return;\n    }\n\n    els.feed.innerHTML = tips\n      .map((t) => {\n        const msg = t.message\n          ? escapeHtml(t.message)\n          : `<span class=\"muted\">(no message)</span>`;\n        return `\n          <li class=\"tip-item\">\n            <div class=\"tip-top\">\n              <span class=\"tip-amount\">${fmtUsdc(t.amount)}</span>\n              <span class=\"tip-time\">${timeAgo(Number(t.timestamp))}</span>\n            </div>\n            <div class=\"tip-msg\">${msg}</div>\n            <div class=\"tip-from\">from ${short(getAddress(t.from))}</div>\n          </li>`;\n      })\n      .join(\"\");\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error(\"feed read failed\", e);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction escapeHtml(s: string): string {\n  return s.replace(/[&<>\"']/g, (c) =>\n    ({ \"&\": \"&amp;\", \"<\": \"&lt;\", \">\": \"&gt;\", '\"': \"&quot;\", \"'\": \"&#39;\" })[c]!,\n  );\n}\n\nasync function refreshWalletBalance(): Promise<void> {\n  if (!account || NOT_DEPLOYED) {\n    els.walletBalance.textContent = \"\";\n    return;\n  }\n  const bal = (await publicClient.readContract({\n    address: USDC,\n    abi: erc20Abi,\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [account],\n  })) as bigint;\n  els.walletBalance.textContent = `Your balance: ${fmtUsdc(bal)}`;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Wallet connection\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync function ensureChain(): Promise<void> {\n  if (!provider) return;\n  const current = await provider.request({ method: \"eth_chainId\" });\n  if (parseInt(current as string, 16) === CHAIN_ID) return;\n\n  const hexId = `0x${CHAIN_ID.toString(16)}`;\n  try {\n    await provider.request({\n      method: \"wallet_switchEthereumChain\",\n      params: [{ chainId: hexId }],\n    });\n  } catch (err) {\n    // 4902 = chain not added to the wallet yet.\n    if ((err as { code?: number }).code === 4902) {\n      await provider.request({\n        method: \"wallet_addEthereumChain\",\n        params: [\n          {\n            chainId: hexId,\n            chainName: activeChain.name,\n            nativeCurrency: activeChain.nativeCurrency,\n            rpcUrls: activeChain.rpcUrls.default.http,\n          },\n        ],\n      });\n    } else {\n      throw err;\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nasync function connect(): Promise<void> {\n  if (!provider) {\n    setStatus(\"error\", \"No injected wallet found. Install MetaMask (or similar) to connect.\");\n    return;\n  }\n  try {\n    setStatus(\"pending\", \"Connecting wallet…\");\n    walletClient = createWalletClient({ chain: activeChain, transport: custom(provider) });\n    const [addr] = await walletClient.requestAddresses();\n    account = addr;\n    await ensureChain();\n    clearStatus();\n    onAccountReady();\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error(e);\n    setStatus(\"error\", (e as Error).message ?? \"Failed to connect wallet.\");\n  }\n}\n\nfunction onAccountReady(): void {\n  els.connect.textContent = account ? short(account) : \"Connect Wallet\";\n  els.networkPill.textContent = activeChain.name;\n  els.networkPill.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n  els.tipBtn.disabled = NOT_DEPLOYED;\n  els.tipBtn.textContent = NOT_DEPLOYED ? \"Contract not deployed\" : \"Send tip\";\n  els.faucetBtn.classList.toggle(\"hidden\", !IS_MOCK || NOT_DEPLOYED);\n  refreshWalletBalance();\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Writes (tip + faucet)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync function sendTip(evt: SubmitEvent): Promise<void> {\n  evt.preventDefault();\n  if (!walletClient || !account) {\n    setStatus(\"error\", \"Connect your wallet first.\");\n    return;\n  }\n\n  let amount: bigint;\n  try {\n    amount = parseUnits(els.amount.value.trim(), USDC_DECIMALS);\n  } catch {\n    setStatus(\"error\", \"Enter a valid amount.\");\n    return;\n  }\n  if (amount <= 0n) {\n    setStatus(\"error\", \"Amount must be greater than zero.\");\n    return;\n  }\n  const message = els.message.value.trim();\n\n  els.tipBtn.disabled = true;\n  try {\n    await ensureChain();\n\n    // 1) Approve the jar to pull USDC, if the current allowance is too low.\n    const allowance = (await publicClient.readContract({\n      address: USDC,\n      abi: erc20Abi,\n      functionName: \"allowance\",\n      args: [account, TIP_JAR],\n    })) as bigint;\n\n    if (allowance < amount) {\n      setStatus(\"pending\", \"Approve USDC spending in your wallet…\");\n      const approveHash = await walletClient.writeContract({\n        address: USDC,\n        abi: erc20Abi,\n        functionName: \"approve\",\n        args: [TIP_JAR, amount],\n        account,\n        chain: activeChain,\n      });\n      setStatus(\"pending\", \"Waiting for approval to confirm…\");\n      await publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: approveHash });\n    }\n\n    // 2) Send the tip.\n    setStatus(\"pending\", \"Confirm the tip in your wallet…\");\n    const tipHash = await walletClient.writeContract({\n      address: TIP_JAR,\n      abi: tipJarAbi,\n      functionName: \"tip\",\n      args: [amount, message],\n      account,\n      chain: activeChain,\n    });\n    setStatus(\"pending\", \"Sending tip…\");\n    await publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: tipHash });\n\n    setStatus(\"success\", `Tipped ${fmtUsdc(amount)}. Thank you! 🎉`);\n    els.form.reset();\n    await Promise.all([refreshStats(), refreshFeed(), refreshWalletBalance()]);\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error(e);\n    setStatus(\"error\", friendlyError(e));\n  } finally {\n    els.tipBtn.disabled = NOT_DEPLOYED;\n  }\n}\n\nasync function useFaucet(): Promise<void> {\n  if (!walletClient || !account) return;\n  els.faucetBtn.disabled = true;\n  try {\n    await ensureChain();\n    setStatus(\"pending\", \"Minting test USDC…\");\n    const hash = await walletClient.writeContract({\n      address: USDC,\n      abi: erc20Abi,\n      functionName: \"mint\",\n      args: [account, parseUnits(\"1000\", USDC_DECIMALS)],\n      account,\n      chain: activeChain,\n    });\n    await publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash });\n    setStatus(\"success\", \"Minted 1,000 test USDC.\");\n    await refreshWalletBalance();\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error(e);\n    setStatus(\"error\", friendlyError(e));\n  } finally {\n    els.faucetBtn.disabled = false;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction friendlyError(e: unknown): string {\n  const msg = (e as { shortMessage?: string; message?: string }).shortMessage\n    ?? (e as Error).message\n    ?? \"Something went wrong.\";\n  if (/user rejected|denied/i.test(msg)) return \"Transaction rejected.\";\n  return msg;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Wire up + boot\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction init(): void {\n  els.contractAddr.textContent = NOT_DEPLOYED ? \"not deployed\" : TIP_JAR;\n  els.tokenAddr.textContent = NOT_DEPLOYED ? \"not deployed\" : USDC;\n\n  if (NOT_DEPLOYED) {\n    setStatus(\"info\", \"No deployment found. Run the deploy script, then reload.\");\n  }\n\n  els.connect.addEventListener(\"click\", connect);\n  els.form.addEventListener(\"submit\", sendTip);\n  els.faucetBtn.addEventListener(\"click\", useFaucet);\n  els.refresh.addEventListener(\"click\", () => {\n    refreshStats();\n    refreshFeed();\n  });\n\n  if (provider) {\n    provider.on?.(\"accountsChanged\", (accs: unknown) => {\n      const list = accs as string[];\n      account = list.length ? getAddress(list[0]) : null;\n      if (!account) {\n        walletClient = null;\n        els.connect.textContent = \"Connect Wallet\";\n        els.tipBtn.disabled = true;\n        els.faucetBtn.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n      } else {\n        onAccountReady();\n      }\n    });\n    provider.on?.(\"chainChanged\", () => window.location.reload());\n  }\n\n  refreshStats();\n  refreshFeed();\n  setInterval(() => {\n    refreshStats();\n    refreshFeed();\n  }, 10_000);\n}\n\ninit();\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

}\n\nbody {\n  margin: 0;\n  font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, -apple-system, \"Segoe UI\", Roboto, sans-serif;\n  background: radial-gradient(1200px 600px at 50% -10%, #1a2547 0%, var(--bg) 60%);\n  color: var(--text);\n  min-height: 100vh;\n}\n\n#app {\n  max-width: 960px;\n  margin: 0 auto;\n  padding: 24px 20px 64px;\n}\n\n.topbar {\n  display: flex;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 16px;\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\n  margin-bottom: 28px;\n}\n.brand { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 14px; }\n.logo { font-size: 40px; line-height: 1; }\nh1 { margin: 0; font-size: 22px; }\n.subtitle { margin: 2px 0 0; color: var(--muted); font-size: 14px; }\n.wallet { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; }\n\n.pill {\n  font-size: 12px;\n  padding: 5px 10px;\n  border-radius: 999px;\n  background: var(--bg-soft);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  color: var(--accent);\n}\n\n.btn {\n  font: inherit;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  border-radius: 10px;\n  padding: 10px 16px;\n  border: 1px solid transparent;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  transition: background 0.15s, opacity 0.15s;\n}\n.btn-primary { background: var(--primary); color: white; }\n.btn-primary:hover:not(:disabled) { background: var(--primary-hover); }\n.btn-primary:disabled { opacity: 0.5; cursor: not-allowed; }\n.btn-ghost {\n  background: transparent;\n  color: var(--accent);\n  border-color: var(--border);\n}\n.btn-ghost:hover:not(:disabled) { background: var(--bg-soft); }\n\n.stats {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, 1fr);\n  gap: 14px;\n  margin-bottom: 24px;\n}\n.stat {\n  background: var(--card);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: 14px;\n  padding: 16px 18px;\n}\n.stat-label { display: block; color: var(--muted); font-size: 13px; }\n.stat-value { display: block; margin-top: 6px; font-size: 22px; font-weight: 700; }\n\n.grid {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;\n  gap: 18px;\n}\n@media (max-width: 760px) {\n  .grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }\n  .stats { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }\n}\n\n.card {\n  background: var(--card);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: 16px;\n  padding: 20px;\n}\n.card h2 { margin: 0 0 16px; font-size: 17px; }\n\nform { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; }\nlabel { font-size: 13px; color: var(--text); margin-top: 6px; }\ninput, textarea {\n  font: inherit;\n  background: var(--bg-soft);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: 10px;\n  color: var(--text);\n  padding: 10px 12px;\n  resize: vertical;\n}\ninput:focus, textarea:focus { outline: 2px solid var(--primary); border-color: transparent; }\nform .btn { margin-top: 10px; }\n.muted { color: var(--muted); font-size: 13px; }\n\n.status {\n  margin-top: 14px;\n  padding: 10px 12px;\n  border-radius: 10px;\n  font-size: 14px;\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n}\n.status-info { background: rgba(96,165,250,0.1); border-color: rgba(96,165,250,0.3); }\n.status-pending { background: rgba(234,179,8,0.1); border-color: rgba(234,179,8,0.3); }\n.status-success { background: rgba(34,197,94,0.12); border-color: rgba(34,197,94,0.4); color: #b7f7cd; }\n.status-error { background: rgba(239,68,68,0.12); border-color: rgba(239,68,68,0.4); color: #ffc9c9; }\n\n.feed-head { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 12px; }\n.feed-head h2 { margin: 0; }\n.feed { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 10px; max-height: 460px; overflow-y: auto; }\n.feed .empty { color: var(--muted); padding: 12px 0; }\n.tip-item {\n  background: var(--bg-soft);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: 12px;\n  padding: 12px 14px;\n}\n.tip-top { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: baseline; }\n.tip-amount { font-weight: 700; color: var(--success); }\n.tip-time { color: var(--muted); font-size: 12px; }\n.tip-msg { margin: 6px 0; font-size: 14px; word-break: break-word; }\n.tip-from { color: var(--muted); font-size: 12px; font-family: ui-monospace, monospace; }\n\nfooter { margin-top: 32px; text-align: center; }\ncode { background: var(--bg-soft); padding: 2px 6px; border-radius: 6px; Override with ?rpc=... if you run anvil elsewhere.\nconst LOCAL_RPC =\n  new URLSearchParams(location.search).get(\"rpc\") ?? \"http://127.0.0.1:8545\";\n\n/** The chain we expect the wallet to be on, ${CHAIN_ID}`,\n        nativeCurrency: { name: \"Ether\", symbol: \"ETH\", decimals: 18 },\n        rpcUrls: { default: { http: [LOCAL_RPC] } },\n      });\n\nconst NOT_DEPLOYED =\n  TIP_JAR === \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\";\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Clients & state\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst publicClient: PublicClient = createPublicClient({\n  chain: activeChain,\n  transport: http(activeChain.rpcUrls.default.http[0]),\n});\n\nlet walletClient: WalletClient | null = null;\nlet account: Address | null = null;\n\nconst provider = (window as unknown as { ethereum?: EIP1193Provider }).ethereum;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// DOM helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst $ = <T extends HTMLElement>(id: string): T =>\n  document.getElementById(id) as T;\n\nconst els = {\n  connect: $(\"connect-btn\") as HTMLButtonElement,\n  networkPill: $(\"network-pill\"),\n  totalTips: $(\"total-tips\"),\n  jarBalance: $(\"jar-balance\"),\n  tipsCount: $(\"tips-count\"),\n  form: $(\"contract-addr\"),\n  tokenAddr: $(\"token-addr\"),\n};\n\nfunction short(addr: string): string {\n  return `${addr.slice(0, 6)}…${addr.slice(-4)}`;\n}\n\nfunction fmtUsdc(v: bigint): string {\n  const n = Number(formatUnits(v, USDC_DECIMALS));\n  return `${n.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 2 })} USDC`;\n}\n\nfunction timeAgo(ts: number): string {\n  const s = Math.max(0,    ? escapeHtml(t.message)\n          : `<span class=\"muted\">(no message)</span>`;\n        return `\n          <li class=\"tip-item\">\n            <div class=\"tip-top\">\n              <span          <div class=\"tip-from\">from ${short(getAddress(t.from))}</div>\n          </li>`;\n      })\n      .join(\"\");\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error(\"feed read failed\", e);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction escapeHtml(s: string): string {\n  return s.replace(/[&<>\"']/g, (c) =>\n    ({ \"&\": \"&amp;\", \"<\": \"&lt;\", \">\": \"&gt;\", '\"': \"&quot;\", \"'\": \"&#39;\" })[c]!,\n  );\n}\n\nasync function refreshWalletBalance(): Promise<void> {\n  if (!account || NOT_DEPLOYED) {\n    els.walletBalance.textContent = \"\";\n    return;\n  }\n  const bal = (await publicClient.readContract({\n    address: USDC,\n    abi: erc20Abi,\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [account],\n  })) as bigint;\n  els.walletBalance.textContent = `Your balance: method: \"eth_chainId\" });\n  if (parseInt(current as string, 16) === CHAIN_ID) return;\n\n  const hexId = `0x${CHAIN_ID.toString(16)}`;\n  try {\n    await provider.request({\n      method: console.error(e);\n    setStatus(\"error\", (e as Error).message ?? \"Failed to connect wallet.\");\n  }\n}\n\nfunction onAccountReady(): void {\n  els.connect.textContent = account ? short(account) : ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Writes (tip + faucet)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nasync function sendTip(evt: SubmitEvent): Promise<void> {\n  evt.preventDefault();\n  if (!walletClient || !account) {\n    setStatus(\"error\", \"Connect your wallet first.\");\n    return;\n  }\n\n  let amount: bigint;\n  try {\n    amount = parseUnits(els.amount.value.trim(), USDC_DECIMALS);\n  } catch {\n    setStatus(\"error\", \"Enter a valid amount.\");\n    return;\n  }\n  if (amount <= 0n) {\n    setStatus(\"error\", \"Amount must be greater than zero.\");\n    return;\n  }\n  const message = els.message.value.trim();\n\n  els.tipBtn.disabled = true;\n  try {\n    await ensureChain();\n\n    // 1) Approve the jar to pull USDC, if the current allowance is too low.\n    const allowance = (await publicClient.readContract({\n      address: USDC,\n      abi: erc20Abi,\n      functionName: \"allowance\",\n      args: [account, TIP_JAR],\n    })) as bigint;\n\n    if (allowance < amount) {\n      setStatus(\"pending\", \"Approve USDC spending in your wallet…\");\n      const approveHash tip.\n    setStatus(\"pending\", \"Confirm the tip in your wallet…\");\n    const tipHash = await walletClient.writeContract({\n      address: TIP_JAR,\n      abi: tipJarAbi,\n      functionName: \"tip\",\n      args: [amount, message],\n      account,\n      chain: activeChain,\n    });\n    setStatus(\"pending\", \"Sending tip…\");\n    await publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash: tipHash });\n\n    setStatus(\"success\", `Tipped ${fmtUsdc(amount)}. Thank you! 🎉`);\n    els.form.reset();\n    await Promise.all([refreshStats(), refreshFeed(), refreshWalletBalance()]);\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error(e);\n    setStatus(\"error\", friendlyError(e));\n  } finally {\n    els.tipBtn.disabled = NOT_DEPLOYED;\n  }\n}\n\nasync function useFaucet(): Promise<void> {\n  if (!walletClient || !account) return;\n  els.faucetBtn.disabled = true;\n  try {\n    await ensureChain();\n    setStatus(\"pending\", \"Minting test USDC…\");\n    const hash = await walletClient.writeContract({\n      address: USDC,\n      abi: erc20Abi,\n      functionName: \"mint\",\n      args: [account, parseUnits(\"1000\", USDC_DECIMALS)],\n      account,\n      chain: activeChain,\n    });\n    await publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash });\n    setStatus(\"success\", \"Minted 1,000 test USDC.\");\n    await refreshWalletBalance();\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error(e);\n    setStatus(\"error\", friendlyError(e));\n  } finally {\n    els.faucetBtn.disabled = false;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction friendlyError(e: unknown): string {\n  const msg = (e as { shortMessage?: string; message?: string }).shortMessage\n    ?? (e as Error).message\n    ?? \"Something went wrong.\";\n  if (/user rejected|denied/i.test(msg)) return \"Transaction rejected.\";\n  return msg;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Wire up + boot\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction init(): void {\n  els.contractAddr.textContent = NOT_DEPLOYED ? \"not deployed\" : TIP_JAR;\n  els.tokenAddr.textContent = NOT_DEPLOYED ? \"not deployed\" : USDC;\n\n  if (NOT_DEPLOYED) {\n    setStatus(\"info\", \"No deployment found. Run the deploy script, then reload.\");\n  }\n\n  els.connect.addEventListener(\"click\", connect);\n  els.form.addEventListener(\"submit\", sendTip);\n  els.faucetBtn.addEventListener(\"click\", useFaucet);\n  els.refresh.addEventListener(\"click\", () => {\n    refreshStats();\n    refreshFeed();\n  });\n\n  if (provider) {\n    provider.on?.(\"accountsChanged\", (accs: unknown) => {\n      const list = accs as string[];\n      account = list.length ? getAddress(list[0]) : null;\n      if (!account) {\n        walletClient = null;\n        els.connect.textContent = \"Connect Wallet\";\n        els.tipBtn.disabled = true;\n        els.faucetBtn.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n      } else {\n        onAccountReady();\n      }\n    });\n    provider.on?.(\"chainChanged\", () => window.location.reload());\n  }\n\n  refreshStats();\n  refreshFeed();\n  setInterval(() => {\n    refreshStats();\n    refreshFeed();\n  }, 10_000);\n}\n\ninit();\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":8,"oldLines":8,"newStart":8,"newLines":6,"lines":["   parseUnits,","   getAddress,","   type Address,","-  type PublicClient,","-  type WalletClient,","   type EIP1193Provider,"," } from \"viem\";"," import { base } from \"viem/chains\";"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

Thank you! 🎉`);\n    els.form.reset();\n    await Promise.all([refreshStats(), refreshFeed(), refreshWalletBalance()]);\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error(e);\n    setStatus(\"error\", friendlyError(e));\n  } finally {\n    els.tipBtn.disabled = NOT_DEPLOYED;\n  }\n}\n\nasync function useFaucet(): Promise<void> {\n  if (!walletClient || !account) return;\n  els.faucetBtn.disabled = true;\n  try {\n    await ensureChain();\n    setStatus(\"pending\", \"Minting test USDC…\");\n    const hash = await walletClient.writeContract({\n      address: USDC,\n      abi: erc20Abi,\n      functionName: \"mint\",\n      args: [account, parseUnits(\"1000\", USDC_DECIMALS)],\n      account,\n      chain: activeChain,\n    });\n    await publicClient.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash });\n    setStatus(\"success\", \"Minted 1,000 test USDC.\");\n    await refreshWalletBalance();\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error(e);\n    setStatus(\"error\", friendlyError(e));\n  } finally {\n    els.faucetBtn.disabled = false;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction friendlyError(e: unknown): string {\n  const msg = (e as { shortMessage?: string; message?: string }).shortMessage\n    ?? (e as Error).message\n    ?? \"Something went wrong.\";\n  if (/user rejected|denied/i.test(msg)) return \"Transaction rejected.\";\n  return msg;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Wire up + boot\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction init(): void {\n  els.contractAddr.textContent = NOT_DEPLOYED ? \"not deployed\" : TIP_JAR;\n  els.tokenAddr.textContent = NOT_DEPLOYED ? \"not deployed\" : USDC;\n\n  if (NOT_DEPLOYED) {\n    setStatus(\"info\", \"No deployment found. Run the deploy script, then reload.\");\n  }\n\n  els.connect.addEventListener(\"click\", connect);\n  els.form.addEventListener(\"submit\", sendTip);\n  els.faucetBtn.addEventListener(\"click\", useFaucet);\n  els.refresh.addEventListener(\"click\", () => {\n    refreshStats();\n    refreshFeed();\n  });\n\n  if (provider) {\n    provider.on?.(\"accountsChanged\", (accs: unknown) => {\n      const list = accs as string[];\n      account = list.length ? getAddress(list[0]) : null;\n      if (!account) {\n        walletClient = null;\n        els.connect.textContent = \"Connect Wallet\";\n        els.tipBtn.disabled = true;\n        els.faucetBtn.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n      } else {\n        onAccountReady();\n      }\n    });\n    provider.on?.(\"chainChanged\", () => window.location.reload());\n  }\n\n  refreshStats();\n  refreshFeed();\n  setInterval(() => {\n    refreshStats();\n    refreshFeed();\n  }, 10_000);\n}\n\ninit();\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":48,"oldLines":12,"newStart":48,"newLines":12,"lines":[" // Clients & state"," // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------"," ","-const publicClient: PublicClient = createPublicClient({","+const publicClient = createPublicClient({","   chain: activeChain,","   transport: http(activeChain.rpcUrls.default.http[0]),"," });"," ","-let walletClient: WalletClient | null = null;","+let walletClient: ReturnType<typeof createWalletClient> | null = null;"," let account: Address | null = null;"," "," const provider = (window as unknown as { ethereum?: EIP1193Provider }).ethereum;"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

Deployment written to ../frontend/src/deployments.json\"\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":776,"cache_read_input_tokens":61169,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":776},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b64972d3-9143-428e-99ba-009c8fd3c974","uuid":"5c3437bf-3c27-4c94-b8e6-bfa3eaf30e41","timestamp":"2026-08-20T00:18:49.389Z","request_id":"req_011CeD3LEa2jMbW8zeRi7yqK"}
